## assistant

The **17th redesign slice is live**. Guided drills now keep separate sittings, resume saved work, and show results for each sitting. I verified three completed sittings on the live site; an early finish showed **0/1 from one answered question**.

The code and verification note are on `main` (`a33fcac`, `df8fd5e`). Focused tests passed **35/35**, along with typecheck and the production build. Lovable’s first publish attempt failed; the retry updated the site, which I then checked while signed in.

The full competitor-audit redesign is **not finished**. Teacher review actions, broader Library and Schedule connections, question study tools, and settings remain open. The [build sequence](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-28-lsatu-build-sequence.md) now records what has shipped and what remains.

# Session Handoff: LSAT U connected experience redesign

## Where it started

Joshua wanted a simpler LSAT U student rail and teacher workspace, informed by detailed audits of LSAT Lab, 7Sage, LSAT Demon, and AdeptLR. The end goal is a coherent student-first experience: fewer main destinations, useful tabs within pages, connected work and review records, dependable practice history, and full teacher controls. Joshua authorized implementation and live deployment in verified slices. He wants Claude to inspect the work, assess what remains, help decide the next priority, and take over.

The complete plan is not implemented. As of 2026-09-29, the running build sequence records 17 verified slices published. Treat the specification and coverage matrix as requirements, not proof that every ticket is complete.

## Decisions locked + what shipped

- Student navigation uses seven main destinations: Home, My Work, Practice, Review, Progress, Library, and Schedule. Practice contains Drills and Practice Exams. My Work combines To do, Completed, and Notes & recaps. Review groups wrong, flagged, skipped, and other review reasons. Progress groups analytics and history.
- Keep direct links and distinct underlying records when destinations are combined. Tabs and locked or unavailable areas must not create misleading pages.
- Build student flows first while retaining the teacher desk. In selected-student Teacher mode, Joshua and admins have full teacher controls under their own identities. A teacher's answers must never count toward the student's attempts, score, completion, or History.
- An assignment remains a distinct instance even if the same drill is assigned twice. Opening it alone does not make it In progress; saved work does. Practice counts as Completed only after a saved finish or submission. Notes & recaps includes labeled teacher notes, session recaps, and student notes.
- Timed work must save answers and place to the student's account. If a save fails, pause the clock and progression, keep a device backup, and show the problem. Older browser-only exam records without a provable owner remain untouched and outside account Attempts.
- Admissions is locked to students while unfinished; Joshua and admins retain access. No new video production is planned.
- The published slices include the short rail, My Work, Review source adapters, Practice Exams navigation, exact LR and RC Inspect flows, first-answer Progress and dated History, connected custom LR and RC history, distinct Smart Drill sittings, and distinct guided drill sittings. Consult the coverage matrix for the precise evidence and remaining checks for each area.
- Most recent code release: commit `a33fcac` on GitHub `main` preserves separate guided drill sittings, saved resume, immutable closeout answers, and stable attempt IDs on retries. The live guided drill page listed three completed teacher-owned test sittings. An early-finish result showed `0 / 1` from one answered question. Focused tests passed 35/35, typecheck and production build passed. Lovable's first publish attempt failed while previewing; a retry updated the site. Commit `df8fd5e` subsequently pushed the live verification note to `main`. The app checkout was clean and matched `origin/main` when checked.
- The final Impeccable pass was deferred by Joshua on 2026-09-29 so functional slices could continue. Do not present the redesign as finished.

## Key files for next session

- Plan first: `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\brainstorms\2026-09-28-lsatu-build-sequence.md` — priority order, blocker links, release notes, and current status.
- Coverage: `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\lsatu-connected-experience\docs\specs\2026-09-28-competitor-audit-coverage.md` — competitor takeaways mapped to tickets, evidence, and incomplete checks.
- Original audit: `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\lsatu-connected-experience\docs\audits\2026-09-27-competitor-ui-audit.md`.

- Clickable student prototype: `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\brainstorms\prototypes\lsatu-student-experience.html`. It is a design reference, not the live app.
- App instructions: `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\lsatu-connected-experience\AGENTS.md`.
- GitHub decision map: https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/155
- GitHub implementation specification: https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/165
- Build tickets: #166 through #180 in that repository. Read their acceptance checks before declaring any ticket complete.

## Running state

- AI OS checkout: `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os`, last checked clean.

## Verification: how to confirm things still work

# AGENTS.md instructions for C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os

<INSTRUCTIONS>
These AGENTS.md instructions replace all previously provided AGENTS.md instructions.

# Global rules — Joshua

These apply on **every** project on this machine, in every session. Project-level
`AGENTS.md` files add to this; they do not replace it.

Keep this file short. It reloads on every message.

---

## Who

Joshua Fisseha — `contact@aspiringattorneys.com`. Builds **LSAT U** (lsatprep.study) and
the Aspiring Attorneys brand. Teaches the LSAT: ~25 one-to-one sessions a week plus
consultations. Windows 11; a MacBook Pro runs some sessions too, so paths differ between
them — never assume `/Users/joshuaf/...` resolves here.

## Where things live

- **AI OS / second brain:** `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` — the Obsidian vault. `context/`,
  `wiki/`, `students/`, `corpus/`, `decisions/`, `brainstorms/`, `skills/`, `projects/`.
  Open this folder rather than starting with no folder, or memory cannot accumulate.
- **Skill catalog:** `dev/ai-os/skills/Codex` (179) and `skills/codex` (154), with
  `PARITY.md` declaring deliberate single-runtime skills and `INDEX.md` cataloging them.
- **LSAT U app:** repo `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db` — React + Vite + TS + Tailwind +
  shadcn, Supabase backend, hosted on Lovable.
- **GitHub:** account `vnjkoxpl-42905` (auto-generated name, correct account).

## How to work with him

- **Do the whole task.** Deliver the finished thing, then say plainly what you skipped
  and why. Don't hand back a plan when the work was asked for.
- **Look before asking.** If the answer is on disk or reachable through a connected tool,
  go get it. He has Firecrawl, Google Workspace (`gws`), and `gh` — use them.
- **Say what's actually true.** Distinguish verified from assumed, every time. If a step
  failed or was skipped, lead with that. A confident wrong answer costs more than "I
  don't know yet."
- **Surface your own mistakes immediately**, including scope errors you notice mid-task.
- Absolute dates (`YYYY-MM-DD`), never "yesterday" or "last week".
- Don't re-ask what he already answered. Don't pad with options he didn't request.

- **Dictation fixes.** A message shaped like `word --> word` (any arrow, any spacing), or
  "save X in my vocabulary", is a Superwhisper correction. Invoke the `superwhisper` skill
  and apply it in that turn, whatever else is in progress.

## Writing as Joshua

Anything published under his name — course copy, lessons, emails, posts.

Plain, direct, confident. Short sentences. Teach the reasoning rather than asserting the
conclusion; this is a teaching brand and the reasoning *is* the product. Concrete beats
abstract. Never condescend to the reader.

**Never use:** "delve into", "dive deep", "unlock", "unleash", "harness the power of",
"in today's fast-paced world", "ever-evolving landscape", "it's not just X, it's Y",
"navigate the complexities", "at the end of the day", "elevate", "supercharge",
"game-changer", "seamless", "robust", "I hope this helps!", "Great question!",
"Certainly!". No em-dash pile-ups. No paragraph opening with "Moreover," /
"Furthermore," / "Additionally,". No emoji in professional writing unless asked.

Headers should say something, not label a category — "Why students miss this", not
"Analysis".

## Safety

- **Draft by default; send only when he says "send".** Email, messages, posts are drafted
  and shown to him. When he says "send it" (or equivalent) in chat, send it, then log it in
  `wiki/send-ledger.md`. Never send on your own initiative, and never treat a request to
  draft as a request to send.
- **Never delete** files, emails, or records without explicit confirmation.
- The Google Workspace grant is broad (it includes deleting all Drive files), so the
  restraint has to live in tool permissions, not in wording. A prompt is not a permission
  layer.
- Secrets stay in `.env` and the OS keyring. Never paste a key into a note, a commit, or
  a chat.

## Tools already connected

- **Firecrawl** — CLI + 28 skills. Prefer it over MCP; the hosted MCP server was removed
  on purpose (the key would sit in plaintext in `~/.Codex.json`).
- **Google Workspace** — `gws` CLI, GCP project `ai-os-joshua`. Gmail, Calendar, Drive,
  Docs, Sheets, Slides, Tasks, People.
- **GitHub** — `gh` CLI, authenticated.

## Cost discipline

`AGENTS.md` reloads on every message, so keep this file lean. Prefer CLIs to MCP servers
(an MCP server can cost ~18k tokens per message in tool definitions). `/clear` between
unrelated tasks. Run the `handoff` skill past ~25% context rather than waiting for
auto-compact.

---

*Written 2026-09-07 from observed behavior. Correct it whenever it's wrong — that is the
point of the file.*

<!-- HAND-OWNED since 2026-07-17. This file is the authority for Codex — edit it directly. On any major change, mirror it into CLAUDE.md so Claude and Codex stay in sync. -->
# Jay 2 — operating instructions for Codex (ai-os)

The canonical root is the repository directory containing this file (`C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` on this Windows machine). Resolve paths from the repository root instead of assuming a saved Mac or Windows absolute path. This is the router: it holds pointers, not knowledge. Read it, then follow the routing map to the right file instead of searching blind. Twin for Claude Code: `CLAUDE.md`.

## Identity and mission

You are **Jay 2**, Joshua's executive assistant. This root is his EA workspace and command center. Joshua is the founder of Aspiring Attorneys and builder of LSAT U (lsatprep.study), a premium LSAT prep platform; he is the product, voice, and content authority. Your job: take operations, admin, drafting, and research off his plate so his time goes to teaching and building. Frame work as decisions and tradeoffs, not basics.

Standing duties, run from here without re-briefing: student operations (transcripts, assignments, follow-ups via `students/`), prospect and consultation correspondence (drafted in his voice, **never auto-sent unless a pre-approved template**), platform actions on lsatprep.study, session-note ingestion from Gmail/Drive, and keeping the wiki ledgers (`tasks`/`hot`/`log`) current so the next session starts oriented.

## Routing map

Paths are relative to the repository root.

| Need | Path |
|---|---|
| **Who Joshua is** (identity, preferences, contact) | `context/about-me.md` |
| **The business** (offers, money, channels, gaps) | `context/work.md` |
| **Current priorities** (the 90-day slate) | `context/priorities.md` |
| **What we decided and why** (locked decisions) | `decisions/log.md` |
| Freshest session state (hot cache) | `wiki/hot.md` |
| Master index · work ledger · dated history | `wiki/index.md` · `wiki/tasks.md` · `wiki/log.md` |
| Product ground truth (pointers to live backends) | `wiki/dashboard.md` |
| LSAT teaching knowledge | `corpus/index.md` |
| A student — profile, transcripts, work, correspondence | `students/<name>/` — start at `students/README.md` |
| Voice for anything a student or prospect reads | `wiki/teaching-voice.md` |
| Was it sent? (every outbound draft/send) | `wiki/send-ledger.md` — append-only; Gmail is authority |
| Who the students are (name, email, sessions, links) | `students/roster.generated.md` — regenerate, never hand-edit |
| Learned facts and preferences (automemory) | native store `~/.claude/projects/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/memory/` + `MEMORY.md` index |
| Skill / agent catalog | `skills/INDEX.md`, `agents/` |
| Systems Jay 2 can reach (registry) | `connections.md` — 7 data domains, mechanism/auth/freshness; read by `/audit` |
| Operator brain (how to think about automation) | `references/3ms-framework.md` — the Three Ms; walked weekly by `/level-up` |
| Automation build backlog (what to build next, ranked) | `references/automation-backlog.md` — corpus-derived gaps + tiers; feeds `/level-up` |
| How-it-works reference notes (methods, not state) | `references/` — also `references/claude-plus-codex.md`, `references/obsidian-command-center.md`, `references/second-brain-levels.md` |
| Web research and Firecrawl agents | `references/firecrawl.md` (guidelines in `references/firecrawl/`) |
| Immutable source evidence · intake queue | `raw/sources/` · `raw/inbox/` — one inbox, one archive |
| Archived web clips, with provenance + hashes | `raw/sources/clips/<slug>/` — clip + `PROVENANCE.md` + `SHA256SUMS` |
| Raw personal-data exports (Google Takeout, Gemini session transcripts) | `raw/data-about-me/` — unprocessed PII, git-ignored; never read wholesale |
| Design/plan thinking, dated (grill-me + interview outputs) | `brainstorms/` — supersedes the retired `aios/` reform folder |
| Session handoffs · chat exports | `handoffs/` — operational records, deliberately OUTSIDE `raw/`; `session-handoff` writes here |
| External teardowns and research, dated | `research/` |
| Web-clipped articles (Obsidian Web Clipper) | `raw/inbox/` — unprocessed only; `ingest` moves them to `raw/sources/clips/` when done |
| Application code | `projects/lsatu/` — own repo, own instructions, own remote |
| Other projects (life-path, …) | `projects/<name>/` — each with its own `CLAUDE.md` + `.claude/` |

## Retrieval order

1. `wiki/hot.md` — current-session narrative and open threads.
2. `wiki/index.md` → the page (teaching knowledge: `corpus/index.md`; a student: their folder). Follow links; there is no semantic search and none is needed at this size.
3. The live system — the final freshness escalation.

**Live systems are authority for operational state; the brain mirrors them:** Cal.com + Google Calendar own sessions scheduled/used, LSAT U Supabase owns homework status/dossier/publish state, Stripe owns payment events, Gmail owns correspondence. On any disagreement, the backend wins. The brain is primary — nothing upstream holds them — for transcripts, teaching notes, judgments, prep briefs, curriculum, product specs, and decisions.

**Do not read the wiki unless the task needs it.** Retrieval costs context every turn. Skip it for: code edits in `projects/lsatu/`; shell/Git/file ops; questions the injected memories already answer; a skill run that names its own inputs; anything mechanical and self-contained. Route to it for: what is X · what did we decide and why · who a student is and where they stand · anything drafted in Joshua's voice for someone else · any decision touching roadmap, pedagogy, or UX.

**Wiki operations (LLM-wiki pattern):** the LLM writes/maintains `wiki/` and `corpus/`; Joshua sources, explores, asks. Raw sources are immutable — read, never modify. *Ingest:* read the source from `raw/`, discuss takeaways, write/update the pages it touches, update `wiki/index.md`, append to `wiki/log.md` (`## [YYYY-MM-DD] ingest | Title`); one source at a time, supervised, unless Joshua says batch. *Query:* index first, then pages; file valuable answers back as pages so explorations compound. *Lint:* `second-brain-lint` for contradictions, stale claims, orphans, missing pages.

## Voice

- **No em-dashes, ever.** Joshua never types them; a stray em-dash reads as AI and costs trust.
- **No AI-slop vocabulary** — never "delve," "tapestry," "testament," "demystify," "boasts," "in the realm of," and their kin.
- **Anything a student or prospect will read** routes through `wiki/teaching-voice.md` and is drafted, never sent, unless it is a pre-approved template.
- **When an outbound message is authorized**, preserve the draft's paragraph breaks, list lines, and intentional spacing. After sending, compare the recipient-facing rendering with the draft and confirm exactly one copy landed. Report any mismatch; never resend automatically.
- **Lead with your recommendation, not a menu.** Be decisive; don't over-hedge.
- **Any prompt Joshua will paste elsewhere** goes inside a fenced code block, never inline prose.

## Tools, keys, and secrets

- **The entire secret model is `.env` + `.gitignore`.** The root `.env` (git-ignored — verify with `git check-ignore .env`) is Joshua's single central store for **everything**: API keys, passwords, and bank/account info (decided 2026-07-17). OAuth client secrets live in `~/.config/gws/`, outside the repo. There is no vault, no Keychain, no encrypted store (`records.sparsebundle` is dead) and none is wanted. Never paste a credential into chat — add it to `.env` directly in the editor.
- **Safety is the tool layer, not prompts:** allow/deny tool lists + scoped API keys. Jay 2 acts under its own credentials and never impersonates Joshua.
- **Connected live systems:** Cal.com, Google Calendar, LSAT U Supabase, Stripe, Gmail. Full registry in `connections.md`.
- **Google access is `gws` CLI only** (`gws gmail|drive|calendar|docs …`, keyring-auth; see `skills/codex/gws-shared/`). Never the Google connector MCPs — the CLI is faster, batchable, and exports Docs to markdown.
- **Government IDs stay outside this OS.** Everything else (student names, emails, schedules, transcripts, notes, payment *links*) is ordinary local working material — classify by content, never by filename.
- **Git remote is enabled.** `origin` is GitHub (`vnjkoxpl-42905/ai-os`, decided 2026-09-29). The outer repo never tracks `.env`, `projects/lsatu/` (own repo), runtime logs, or locks. Do not create gitlinks.
- **New laptop restore:** GitHub contains tracked ai-os files and skills. It deliberately excludes `.env`, `raw/`, app repos, credentials, and machine sessions. Restore machine-specific files from the private backup using `setup/backup/README.md` and its `README - START HERE.md`; install tools separately and sign in again where sessions cannot be restored. Never move credentials, raw personal data, or active browser tokens into GitHub.

## Skills

Everything is a skill — **there are no slash commands** (decided 2026-07-17: one kind, one home, one activation path). The catalog at `skills/` and `agents/` is inert storage; nothing auto-loads. An item runs only once **activated by copy** (not symlink) into `.agents/skills/` (Claude: `.claude/skills/`). Creating one writes to **both** the project and the catalog (`skills/{claude,codex}/`), project-scoped — never auto-global. `claude/` and `codex/` subtrees are never merged; a vendor item is a live symlink + a frozen `_snapshots/` copy. `system/scripts/check-skills.sh` validates the model; `skills/INDEX.md` indexes it.

Active in this root. Run `pickup` only when Joshua explicitly asks for it. Do not run it automatically at session start. Use `wrapup` at the end of substantive sessions:

| Skill | Use it when |
|---|---|
| `pickup` / `wrapup` | `pickup` only on Joshua's explicit request / `wrapup` at session end (wrapup persists to the wiki ledgers) |
| `session-handoff` | mid-work context reset near ~250k, or passing work Codex↔Claude — chat-only, writes nothing |
| `audit` | scoring the OS against the Four Cs (structural health, /100) |
| `level-up` | weekly 3Ms interview to find + ship one automation |
| `consultation-inquiry-drafts`, `-followup-drafts`, `inbound-tutoring-replies` | drafting to prospects/students — always via the teaching voice |
| `guided-drill-assignment` | assigning a student a drill |
| `lsat-u-platform-actions` | doing anything ON lsatprep.study from here (assign, post, message, ingest) |
| `tutorocean-profile-fill` | TutorOcean profile work |
| `prompt-architect-refiner` | refining a prompt before it ships |
| `accommodations-email` | "accommodations email to X" (dictation: "accommodations scale"); pre-approved send, QP form attached, self-logging |

When a skill run exposes a gap, improve the skill before moving on. Runtime-specific catalog differences must be justified in `skills/PARITY.md`; undeclared differences are defects.

## Guardrails

- **95% rule.** Make no changes until you are 95% confident what Joshua wants. Ask until you are.
- **Lovable credits require confirmation.** Never take an action that spends or may spend Lovable credits without Joshua's explicit confirmation immediately before the action. Do not infer approval from a broader request to fix, build, deploy, or continue. Free Lovable controls and direct deployment paths may be used without this credit-spend confirmation.
- **Lovable Cloud deploys through Lovable.** LSAT U currently uses a Lovable Cloud-managed backend. Deploy its Edge Functions through the Lovable agent, not the Supabase CLI, unless the backend has first been migrated to a separately managed Supabase project. Confirm this route with Joshua before starting a Lovable deployment.
- **Root stays thin.** Don't accumulate files at the root; work products go to their homes (wiki, students, corpus, projects). Anything left at root gets filed before the session ends.
- **Delegate bulk work.** Exploration or analysis spanning 3+ files goes to a sub-agent that returns summaries, not file dumps.
- **Shell ergonomics.** Run one command per call — chained `cmd; cmd` and `rm -rf X && clone Y` forms trip permission denials that the split commands would not. Never poll with `sleep`. Use backgrounded execution for anything slow, and raise the timeout explicitly for known-slow ops (large moves, typecheck-gated pushes, full verify runs) rather than eating the default.
- **Search before creating.** One authoritative page per subject — update it, don't grow a parallel truth. `wiki/index.md` catalogs every page and updates on every ingest.
- **Preserve provenance and source hashes.** Never move, delete, or modify a migration source root — copy and hash-verify. Never follow a source symlink outside its registered root.
- **Conflicts stay explicit until adjudicated** — never silently overwrite one source generation with another.
- **Automation writes only generated files or fenced generated sections,** through compare-and-swap.
- **Obey nested instruction files** — they win over broader ones for their subtree. LSATU's recovered `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` are inviolate: never regenerate them without Joshua's explicit approval.
- **Memory = native automemory.** "Remember that…" writes one fact per file to the native store; `MEMORY.md` indexes it. This file is the rules; memory is learned facts — never mix them. Transcripts and raw captures never auto-promote.
- **Append meaningful system changes** to `wiki/log.md`; log locked decisions to `decisions/log.md`.

## Dual-runtime (Codex + Claude)

- Begin at the Git-root `AGENTS.md` and follow directory-scoped instruction files toward the working path; the nearest file wins for its subtree.
- Keep root instructions inside the runtime byte budget. Move narrow rules to the narrowest owning directory rather than growing this file.
- Activated skills are **copies** in `.agents/skills/` (from `skills/codex/`); agent definitions convert to `.codex/agents/*.toml` with the instructions in `developer_instructions`.
- Claude reads `CLAUDE.md` and never this file. Anything that must hold for both runtimes goes in BOTH files — when you change it here, mirror it there; keep the routing map and guardrails byte-identical between them.
- Activation is unequal by design: Codex reads copies in `.agents/skills/`, Claude reads copies in `.claude/skills/`. Codex runs the catalog minus the wiki/Obsidian and `graphify` skills, so the two skill tables legitimately differ.
- Never have Claude and Codex editing the same file at once; pass work between them by session handoff (summarize → clear → paste). At ~250k tokens, hand off rather than riding autocompact.

</INSTRUCTIONS>

`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.

When you provide a response in the final channel, that content is immediately delivered back to your parent agent.
In addition, your final answer may be read by a human, so ensure it is legible.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: NEW_TASK | MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>

<payload text>
```
You may also see them addressed as to=/root/..., which indicates your identity is /root/...

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. A pushed "fix" that does not hold costs a second full session; this has already happened once.\r\n\r\n## Workflow Expectations\r\n\r\n- Run `npm run build` before wrapping up significant code or documentation work.\r\n- If tests are relevant to the change, run them too.\r\n- After completing a change, stage the intended files, commit with a concise message, and push to `origin main` unless the user says otherwise.\r\n- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session, so a raw push can be rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges `origin/main` if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes. It also gates on `npm run typecheck`.\r\n- **Never roll `main` back.** Do not use Lovable\'s "Revert to commit", "revert and resend", or history restore, and never reset or force-push `main`. Emit a 6-8 line briefing (where we left off, Now, Next, open threads, hot, suggested next) and wait. Do not deep-read other pages until pointed at a task.\r\n- `wrapup` — end of session. [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 2.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "/**\r\n * Fire-and-forget logger for Intro to LR per-question attempts.\r\n *\r\n * Every answer the student submits in the bootcamp's InteractiveDrillRunner\r\n * is recorded to `public.intro_lr_attempts`, scoped to the signed-in user.\r\n * Errors are swallowed (with a console.warn) so a bad network never breaks\r\n * the drill UI.\r\n */\r\n\r\nimport { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';\r\n\r\nexport interface IntroLrAttemptInput {\r\n  drillId: string;\r\n  questionId: string;\r\n  mode: string;\r\n  correct: boolean;\r\n  selectedAnswer?: string | null;\r\n  timeMs?: number | null;\r\n}\r\n\r\n// In-memory de-dupe so re-renders or accidental double-fires don't double-log\r\n// the same question response within a session.\r\nconst seen = new Set<string>();\r\n\r\nexport function logIntroLrAttempt(input: IntroLrAttemptInput): void {\r\n  void (async () => {\r\n    try {\r\n      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\r\n      if (!user) return;\r\n      const dedupeKey = `${user.id}::${input.drillId}::${input.questionId}::${input.correct}::${input.selectedAnswer ?? ''}`;\r\n      if (seen.has(dedupeKey)) return;\r\n      seen.add(dedupeKey);\r\n\r\n      const { error } = await supabase.from('intro_lr_attempts').insert({\r\n        user_id: user.id,\r\n        drill_id: input.drillId,\r\n        question_id: input.questionId,\r\n        mode: input.mode,\r\n        selected_answer: input.selectedAnswer ?? null,\r\n        correct: input.correct,\r\n        time_ms: input.timeMs ?? null,\r\n      });\r\n      if (error) {\r\n        console.warn('[intro_lr_attempts] insert failed', error.message);\r\n        seen.delete(dedupeKey);\r\n      }\r\n    } catch (e) {\r\n      console.warn('[intro_lr_attempts] log threw', e);\r\n    }\r\n  })();\r\n}\r\n"}]

\'D34-S4-Q5\': [\'conclusion\', \'concession\', \'support\'],\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport function splitStimulusIntoParts(stimulus: string, prefix: string): StimulusPart[] {\r\n  return stimulus\r\n    .split(/(?<=[.!?])\\s+/)\r\n    .map((text) => text.trim())\r\n    .filter(Boolean)\r\n    .map((text, index) => ({ id: `${prefix}-part-${index + 1}`, text }));\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction clean(text: string): string | undefined {\r\n  const target = clean(text);\r\n  return parts.find((part) => clean(part.text).includes(target) || target.includes(clean(part.text)))?.id;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction roleLabel(role: PartRole): string {\r\n  switch (role) {\r\n    case \'main\':\r\n      return \'Main Conclusion\';\r\n    case \'intermediate\':\r\n      return \'Intermediate Conclusion\';\r\n    case \'premise\':\r\n    case \'support\':\r\n      return \'Support\';\r\n    case \'opposing\':\r\n      return \'Opposing View\';\r\n    case \'concession\':\r\n      return \'Concession\';\r\n    case \'background\':\r\n      return \'Background\';\r\n    default:\r\n      return \'Conclusion\';\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction revealFromParts(parts: StimulusPart[]): RevealEntry[] {\r\n  return parts\r\n    .filter((part) => part.role)\r\n    .map((part) => ({\r\n      id: part.id,\r\n      role: part.role!,\r\n      label: roleLabel(part.role!),\r\n      text: part.text,\r\n      note: part.hint,\r\n    }));\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function adaptIndicatorQuestion(q: IndicatorIdQuestion): InteractiveDrillItem {\r\n  return {\r\n    id: q.id,\r\n    mode: \'signal_classification\',\r\n    title: \'Signal Classification\',\r\n    prompt: q.prompt,\r\n    payload: q,\r\n    stimulus: q.text,\r\n    hint: `Ask what the highlighted phrase introduces: support, the author\'s claim, a turn, a concession, or someone else\'s view.`,\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function adaptXRayQuestion(q: XRayQuestion): InteractiveDrillItem {\r\n  const parts = q.candidates.map((candidate, index) => ({\r\n    id: `${q.id}-part-${index + 1}`,\r\n    text: candidate.text,\r\n    role: candidate.is_correct ? \'conclusion\' as PartRole : inferSupportRole(candidate.text),\r\n    hint: candidate.is_correct ? q.rationale : \'This sentence is doing work for, against, or around the author’s main point.\',\r\n  }));\r\n  const correct = parts.find((part) => part.role === \'conclusion\');\r\n  return {\r\n    id: q.id,\r\n    mode: \'conclusion_click\',\r\n    title: \'Structure Reading\',\r\n    prompt: q.prompt,\r\n    payload: q,\r\n    stimulus: q.stimulus,\r\n    parts,\r\n    correctConclusionPartId: correct?.id,\r\n    correctSupportPartIds: parts.filter((part) => part.role === \'support\' || part.role === \'premise\').map((part) => part.id),\r\n    correctOpposingPartIds: parts.filter((part) => part.role === \'opposing\').map((part) => part.id),\r\n    hint: \'Find the claim the other sentences are trying to prove. Facts and examples usually support; pivots often introduce the author’s real view.\',\r\n    revealMap: revealFromParts(parts),\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function adaptFirstSentenceQuestion(q: FirstSentenceQuestion): InteractiveDrillItem {\r\n  const roleSequence = EXPLICIT_ROLE_SEQUENCES[q.id];\r\n  const parts = splitStimulusIntoParts(q.stimulus, q.id).map((part, index) => {\r\n    const explicitRole = roleSequence?.[index];\r\n    const isConclusion = explicitRole\r\n      ? explicitRole === \'conclusion\' || explicitRole === \'main\'\r\n      : q.is_first_sentence_conclusion ? index === 0 : conclusionIndexFromRationale(q.rationale, index);\r\n    return {\r\n      ...part,\r\n      role: explicitRole ?? (isConclusion ? \'conclusion\' as PartRole : inferSupportRole(part.text, q.rationale)),\r\n      hint: index === 0 && !q.is_first_sentence_conclusion ? firstSentenceTrapLabel(q.rationale) : undefined,\r\n    };\r\n  });\r\n  const correct = parts.find((part) => part.role === \'conclusion\') ?? parts[0];\r\n  return {\r\n    id: q.id,\r\n    mode: \'first_sentence_check\',\r\n    title: \'First-Sentence Check\',\r\n    prompt: q.prompt,\r\n    payload: q,\r\n    stimulus: q.stimulus,\r\n    parts,\r\n    correctConclusionPartId: correct?.id,\r\n    hint: \'First click the author’s actual conclusion. Then decide whether that conclusion was the first sentence.\',\r\n    revealMap: revealFromParts(parts),\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function adaptFamilyQuestion(q: FamilyQuestion): InteractiveDrillItem {\r\n  const roleSequence = EXPLICIT_ROLE_SEQUENCES[q.id];\r\n  const parts = splitStimulusIntoParts(q.stimulus, q.id).map((part, index) => {\r\n    const role = roleSequence?.[index] ?? roleForFamilyPart(q, part.text, index);\r\n    return { ...part, role, hint: roleLabel(role) };\r\n  });\r\n  const conclusion = parts.find((part) => part.role === \'conclusion\' || part.role === \'main\');\r\n  return {\r\n    id: q.id,\r\n    mode: \'rebuttal_dissection\',\r\n    title: \'Rebuttal Dissection\',\r\n    prompt: q.prompt,\r\n    payload: q,\r\n    stimulus: q.stimulus,\r\n    parts,\r\n    correctConclusionPartId: conclusion?.id,\r\n    correctSupportPartIds: parts.filter((part) => part.role === \'support\' || part.role === \'premise\').map((part) => part.id),\r\n    correctOpposingPartIds: parts.filter((part) => part.role === \'opposing\' || part.role === \'concession\').map((part) => part.id),\r\n    hint: \'Separate the author’s claim from the view being rejected. Rebuttal items often start with someone else’s view.\',\r\n    revealMap: revealFromParts(parts),\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function adaptChainQuestion(q: ChainMappingQuestion): InteractiveDrillItem {\r\n  const parts = splitStimulusIntoParts(q.stimulus, q.id).map((part) => {\r\n    const role = roleForChainPart(q, part.text);\r\n    return { ...part, role };\r\n  });\r\n  return {\r\n    id: q.id,\r\n    mode: \'chain_mapping\',\r\n    title: \'Chain Mapping\',\r\n    prompt: q.prompt,\r\n    payload: q,\r\n    stimulus: q.stimulus,\r\n    parts,\r\n    correctConclusionPartId: findPartId(parts, q.layers.main_conclusion),\r\n    correctIntermediateConclusionPartIds: q.layers.intermediate_conclusion ? [findPartId(parts, q.layers.intermediate_conclusion)].filter(Boolean) as string[] : [],\r\n    correctSupportPartIds: q.layers.premises.map((premise) => findPartId(parts, premise)).filter(Boolean) as string[],\r\n    hint: \'A premise supports something. An intermediate conclusion is supported by a premise and then supports the main conclusion.\',\r\n    revealMap: revealFromParts(parts),\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function adaptNestedQuestion(q: NestedClaimsQuestion): InteractiveDrillItem {\r\n  const parts = q.candidates.map((candidate, index) => ({\r\n    id: `${q.id}-part-${index + 1}`,\r\n    text: candidate.text,\r\n    role: candidate.role === \'main\' ? \'main\' as PartRole : candidate.role,\r\n  }));\r\n  return {\r\n    id: q.id,\r\n    mode: \'nested_claims\',\r\n    title: \'Nested Claims\',\r\n    prompt: q.prompt,\r\n    payload: q,\r\n    stimulus: q.stimulus,\r\n    parts,\r\n    correctConclusionPartId: parts.find((part) => part.role === \'main\')?.id,\r\n    correctIntermediateConclusionPartIds: parts.filter((part) => part.role === \'intermediate\').map((part) => part.id),\r\n    correctSupportPartIds: parts.filter((part) => part.role === \'premise\').map((part) => part.id),\r\n    hint: \'Do not stop at the intermediate claim. The main conclusion is the destination the whole chain supports.\',\r\n    revealMap: revealFromParts(parts),\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction inferSupportRole(text: string, rationale = \'\'): PartRole {\r\n  const combined = `${text} ${rationale}`.toLowerCase();\r\n  if (/(many believe|critics|some|opposing view|concession|granted|it is true|while)/.test(combined)) return \'opposing\';\r\n  if (/(background|rhetorical question|sets up)/.test(combined)) return \'background\';\r\n  return \'support\';\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction conclusionIndexFromRationale(rationale: string, index: number): boolean {\r\n  const lower = rationale.toLowerCase();\r\n  if (/(final sentence|third sentence|conclusion is the final)/.test(lower)) return index === 2;\r\n  if (/(sentence two|second sentence|follows in sentence two)/.test(lower)) return index === 1;\r\n  if (/(this is false|evidence does not|doctors should|honest answer|variance should)/.test(lower)) return index > 0;\r\n  return index === 1;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction firstSentenceTrapLabel(rationale: string): string {\r\n  const lower = rationale.toLowerCase();\r\n  if (lower.includes(\'opposing\')) return \'First sentence is the rejected view.\';\r\n  if (lower.includes(\'concession\')) return \'First sentence is a concession.\';\r\n  if (lower.includes(\'support\') || lower.includes(\'statistic\')) return \'First sentence is support.\';\r\n  if (lower.includes(\'rhetorical\')) return \'First sentence is setup.\';\r\n  return \'First sentence is not the author’s final claim.\';\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction roleForFamilyPart(q: FamilyQuestion, text: string, index: number): PartRole {\r\n  const lower = `${text} ${q.rationale}`.toLowerCase();\r\n  if (q.family === \'First-sentence\') return index === 0 ? \'conclusion\' : \'support\';\r\n  if (index === 0) return lower.includes(\'granted\') || lower.includes(\'it is true\') ? \'concession\' : \'opposing\';\r\n  if (/(wrong|mistaken|opposite|cannot|does not support|reply|truth|should not|does not back|actual)/.test(lower)) return \'conclusion\';\r\n  return \'support\';\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction roleForChainPart(q: ChainMappingQuestion, text: string): PartRole {\r\n  const normalized = clean(text);\r\n  if (normalized.includes(clean(q.layers.main_conclusion)) || clean(q.layers.main_conclusion).includes(normalized)) return \'main\';\r\n  if (q.layers.intermediate_conclusion) {\r\n    const intermediate = clean(q.layers.intermediate_conclusion);\r\n    if (normalized.includes(intermediate) || intermediate.includes(normalized)) return \'intermediate\';\r\n  }\r\n  return \'premise\';\r\n}\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.6 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'import { rcDb, RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE, RC_SESSIONS_TABLE, type RcAttemptRow, type RcDrillSessionRow } from "@/features/rc/api/rcSupabase.types";\r\nimport { loadRcQuestionIndex } from "@/features/rc/lib/rcBank";\r\nimport { buildRcReviewQueue } from "./rcReviewAdapter";\r\n\r\nconst PAGE_SIZE = 500;\r\n\r\nasync function ownedRows<T>(table: string, userId: string, dateColumn: string): Promise<T[]> {\r\n  const rows: T[] = [];\r\n  for (let start = 0; ; start += PAGE_SIZE) {\r\n    const { data, error } = await rcDb.from(table)\r\n      .select("*")\r\n      .eq("user_id", userId)\r\n      .order(dateColumn, { ascending: true })\r\n      .order("id", { ascending: true })\r\n      .range(start, start + PAGE_SIZE - 1);\r\n    if (error) throw error;\r\n    const page = (data ?? []) as T[];\r\n    rows.push(...page);\r\n    if (page.length < PAGE_SIZE) return rows;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport async function loadRcReviewQueue(userId: string) {\r\n  const [sessions, attempts, index] = await Promise.all([\r\n    ownedRows<RcDrillSessionRow>(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE, userId, "started_at"),\r\n    ownedRows<RcAttemptRow>(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE, userId, "created_at"),\r\n    loadRcQuestionIndex(),\r\n  ]);\r\n  return buildRcReviewQueue(attempts, sessions, index.questions);\r\n}\r\n'}]

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-connected-experience/src/pages/IntroLrReviewDetail.tsx\n+import * as React from \"react\";\n+import { ArrowLeft } from \"lucide-react\";\n+import { useNavigate, useParams, useSearchParams } from \"react-router-dom\";\n+import { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\n+import { useAuth } from \"@/contexts/AuthContext\";\n+import { fetchOwnedIntroLrAttempt, getAuthoredIntroLrQuestion, type IntroLrAttempt } from \"@/components/review/introLrReviewSource\";\n+import type { InteractiveDrillItem } from \"@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/components/interactive-drills/types\";\n+\n+function savedAnswer(item: InteractiveDrillItem, raw: string | null): string {\n+  if (!raw) return \"No answer was recorded for this attempt.\";\n+  if (item.mode === \"conclusion_click\" || item.mode === \"rebuttal_dissection\") {\n+    const parts = raw.split(\":\");\n+    return parts.map((part) => item.parts?.find((candidate) => candidate.id === part)?.text ?? part).join(\" · \");\n+  }\n+  if (item.mode === \"first_sentence_check\") return raw === \"true\" ? \"Yes, the conclusion was the first sentence\" : raw === \"false\" ? \"No, the conclusion was not the first sentence\" : raw;\n+  if (item.mode === \"chain_mapping\" || item.mode === \"nested_claims\") {\n+    try {\n+      const roles = JSON.parse(raw) as Record<string, string>;\n+      if (roles && typeof roles === \"object\" && !Array.isArray(roles)) return Object.entries(roles)\n+        .map(([id, role]) => `${item.parts?.find((part) => part.id === id)?.text ?? id}: ${role}`).join(\"\\n\");\n+    } catch { /* Old answers may be plain text. */ }\n+  }\n+  return raw;\n+}\n+\n+function reasoning(item: InteractiveDrillItem): string {\n+  const payload = item.payload as { rationale?: string; layers?: { main_conclusion: string; intermediate_conclusion?: string; premises: string[] } };\n+  if (payload.rationale) return payload.rationale;\n+  if (payload.layers) return `Main conclusion: ${payload.layers.main_conclusion}. ${payload.layers.intermediate_conclusion ? `Intermediate conclusion: ${payload.layers.intermediate_conclusion}. ` : \"\"}Support: ${payload.layers.premises.join(\"; \")}.`;\n+  return \"The authored answer map below shows the intended structure.\";\n+}\n+\n+export default function IntroLrReviewDetail() {\n+  const { attemptId } = useParams();\n+  const { user } = useAuth();\n+  const navigate = useNavigate();\n+  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();\n+  const requestedReturn = searchParams.get(\"returnTo\");\n+  const returnTo = requestedReturn?.split(\"?\")[0] === \"/review/to-review\"\n+    ? requestedReturn : \"/review/to-review?reason=missed\";\n+  const [attempt, setAttempt] = React.useState<IntroLrAttempt | null>(null);\n+  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(true);\n+  const [error, setError] = React.useState<string | null>(null);\n+\n+  React.useEffect(() => {\n+    if (!user?.id || !attemptId) {\n+      setLoading(false);\n+      setError(\"This review link is invalid.\");\n+      return;\n+    }\n+    let active = true;\n+    setLoading(true);\n+    setError(null);\n+    void fetchOwnedIntroLrAttempt(user.id, attemptId).then((row) => {\n+      if (active) setAttempt(row);\n+    }).catch(() => {\n+      if (active) setError(\"This saved exercise attempt could not be loaded. Try again later.\");\n+    }).finally(() => { if (active) setLoading(false); });\n+    return () => { active = false; };\n+  }, [user?.id, attemptId]);\n+\n+  const question = attempt ? getAuthoredIntroLrQuestion(attempt.question_id) : null;\n+  return <div className=\"mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl px-4 py-7 sm:px-6\">\n+    <Button variant=\"ghost\" type=\"button\" onClick={() => navigate(returnTo)} className=\"mb-5 min-h-11\">\n+      <ArrowLeft className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" aria-hidden /> Back to Review\n+    </Button>\n+    {loading ? <p role=\"status\">Loading saved exercise attempt…</p>\n+      : error ? <p role=\"alert\" className=\"text-destructive\">{error}</p>\n+      : !attempt || !question ? <p role=\"status\">This Intro to LR attempt is not available for review in your account.</p>\n+      : <article className=\"space-y-6\">\n+        <header>\n+          <p className=\"text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground\">Intro to LR · Missed exercise attempt</p>\n+          <h1 className=\"mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold\">{question.title}</h1>\n+          <p className=\"mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground\">{attempt.question_id} · {new Date(attempt.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</p>\n+        </header>\n+        <section className=\"space-y-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-5\" aria-label=\"Authored exercise\">\n+          <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold\">The exercise</h2>\n+          <p>{question.prompt}</p>\n+          {question.stimulus && <p className=\"whitespace-pre-wrap\">{question.stimulus}</p>}\n+          {question.parts?.length ? <ol className=\"list-decimal space-y-2 pl-5\">{question.parts.map((part) => <li key={part.id}>{part.text}</li>)}</ol> : null}\n+          {question.mode === \"signal_classification\" && \"highlighted_word\" in question.payload ? <p>Highlighted phrase: <strong>{String(question.payload.highlighted_word)}</strong></p> : null}\n+        </section>\n+        <section className=\"space-y-2 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-5\" aria-label=\"Saved answer\">\n+          <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold\">Your saved answer</h2>\n+          <p className=\"whitespace-pre-wrap\">{savedAnswer(question, attempt.selected_answer)}</p>\n+          {attempt.selected_answer === null && <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">The original exercise marked this attempt incorrect, but did not store the answer text.</p>}\n+        </section>\n+        <section className=\"space-y-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-5\" aria-label=\"Authored reasoning\">\n+          <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold\">How to reason through it</h2>\n+          <p>{reasoning(question)}</p>\n+          {question.revealMap?.length ? <ul className=\"space-y-2\">{question.revealMap.map((entry) => <li key={entry.id}><strong>{entry.label}:</strong> {entry.text}</li>)}</ul> : null}\n+          {question.mode === \"signal_classification\" && \"correct_category\" in question.payload ? <p>Correct category: <strong>{String(question.payload.correct_category)}</strong></p> : null}\n+          {question.mode === \"first_sentence_check\" && \"is_first_sentence_conclusion\" in question.payload ? <p>Was the conclusion first? <strong>{question.payload.is_first_sentence_conclusion ? \"Yes\" : \"No\"}</strong></p> : null}\n+        </section>\n+        <p className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">The answer and outcome are from this saved attempt. Exercise wording and reasoning are from the current authored content. Viewing this page does not create another attempt.</p>\n+      </article>}\n+  </div>;\n+}\n*** End Patch"))

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-connected-experience/src/components/review/introLrReviewSource.test.ts\n+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from \"vitest\";\n+import type { IntroLrAttempt } from \"./introLrReviewSource\";\n+\n+const { from, insert } = vi.hoisted(() => ({ from: vi.fn(), insert: vi.fn() }));\n+vi.mock(\"@/integrations/supabase/client\", () => ({ supabase: { from } }));\n+\n+import { buildIntroLrReviewQueue, fetchOwnedIntroLrAttempt, getAuthoredIntroLrQuestion, loadIntroLrReviewQueue } from \"./introLrReviewSource\";\n+\n+const attempt = (id: string, overrides: Partial<IntroLrAttempt> = {}): IntroLrAttempt => ({\n+  id, user_id: \"student-1\", drill_id: \"MC-DRL-3.3\", question_id: \"D33-S1-Q1\", mode: \"first_sentence_check\",\n+  selected_answer: \"false\", correct: false, time_ms: null, created_at: \"2026-09-28T12:00:00Z\", ...overrides,\n+});\n+\n+describe(\"Intro to LR Review source\", () => {\n+  beforeEach(() => { from.mockReset(); insert.mockReset(); });\n+\n+  it(\"keeps repeated missed attempts separate and excludes unknown or correct answers\", () => {\n+    const rows = [attempt(\"a\"), attempt(\"b\", { selected_answer: null }), attempt(\"retired\", { question_id: \"retired\" }), attempt(\"correct\", { correct: true })];\n+    const items = buildIntroLrReviewQueue(rows);\n+    expect(items.map((item) => item.key)).toEqual([\"intro-lr:a\", \"intro-lr:b\"]);\n+    expect(items[1].firstAnswer).toBeNull();\n+    expect(getAuthoredIntroLrQuestion(\"retired\")).toBeNull();\n+  });\n+\n+  it(\"queries only the signed-in owner's missed rows\", async () => {\n+    const filters: Array<[string, unknown]> = [];\n+    const chain = {\n+      select: vi.fn().mockReturnThis(), eq: vi.fn((name: string, value: unknown) => { filters.push([name, value]); return chain; }),\n+      order: vi.fn().mockReturnThis(), range: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ data: [attempt(\"a\")], error: null }),\n+      insert,\n+    };\n+    from.mockReturnValue(chain);\n+    expect(await loadIntroLrReviewQueue(\"student-1\")).toHaveLength(1);\n+    expect(filters).toContainEqual([\"user_id\", \"student-1\"]);\n+    expect(filters).toContainEqual([\"correct\", false]);\n+    expect(from).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\"intro_lr_attempts\");\n+    expect(insert).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n+  });\n+\n+  it(\"fetches a single exact owned miss, never writing on review\", async () => {\n+    const filters: Array<[string, unknown]> = [];\n+    const chain = {\n+      select: vi.fn().mockReturnThis(), eq: vi.fn((name: string, value: unknown) => { filters.push([name, value]); return chain; }),\n+      maybeSingle: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: null }), insert,\n+    };\n+    from.mockReturnValue(chain);\n+    expect(await fetchOwnedIntroLrAttempt(\"student-1\", \"someone-elses-row\")).toBeNull();\n+    expect(filters).toEqual([[\"id\", \"someone-elses-row\"], [\"user_id\", \"student-1\"], [\"correct\", false]]);\n+    expect(insert).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n+  });\n+\n+  it(\"reports read failures instead of an empty successful queue\", async () => {\n+    const chain = {\n+      select: vi.fn().mockReturnThis(), eq: vi.fn().mockReturnThis(), order: vi.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n+      range: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: new Error(\"network unavailable\") }),\n+    };\n+    from.mockReturnValue(chain);\n+    await expect(loadIntroLrReviewQueue(\"student-1\")).rejects.toThrow(\"network unavailable\");\n+  });\n+});\n*** End Patch"))

\'?\'}/run`}\r\n        />\r\n      </div>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  let content: ReactNode;\r\n  switch (drill.slug) {\r\n    case \'main-conclusion-lab\':\r\n      content = <MainConclusionLab />;\r\n      break;\r\n    case \'first-sentence-vs-rebuttal-lab\':\r\n      content = <FirstSentenceVsRebuttalLab drillId={drill.completionId} title={drill.title} />;\r\n      break;\r\n    case \'conclusion-type-lab\':\r\n      content = <ConclusionTypeLab />;\r\n      break;\r\n    default:\r\n      content = (\r\n        <EmptyState\r\n          title="Drill not found"\r\n          body="Pick a drill from the Drill Lab to get started."\r\n          surfaceId={`drill/${drillId ?? \'?\'}/run`}\r\n        />\r\n      );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (drill.slug === \'main-conclusion-lab\') {\r\n    return (\r\n      <div className="min-h-screen bg-[radial-gradient(circle_at_50%_0%,rgba(6,182,212,0.10)_0%,transparent_50%),linear-gradient(rgba(255,255,255,0.03)_1px,transparent_1px),linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(255,255,255,0.03)_1px,transparent_1px)] bg-[length:100%_100%,20px_20px,20px_20px] bg-[#0b1120] px-5 py-5 md:px-8">\r\n        <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1200px]">\r\n          <div className="mb-5">\r\n            <Link\r\n              to={drill.route}\r\n              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.10)] bg-[rgba(15,23,42,0.68)] px-4 py-2 font-mc-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#94a3b8] transition-colors hover:bg-[rgba(15,23,42,0.84)] hover:text-white"\r\n            >\r\n              <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />\r\n              Back to Drill Page\r\n            </Link>\r\n          </div>\r\n          <div className="min-w-0">{content}</div>\r\n        </div>\r\n      </div>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (drill.slug === \'first-sentence-vs-rebuttal-lab\' || drill.slug === \'conclusion-type-lab\') {\r\n    return <>{content}</>;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1200px] px-6 py-8 md:px-10 md:py-10 desktop:px-12 desktop:py-12">\r\n      <div className="mb-6">\r\n        <Link\r\n          to={drill.route}\r\n          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-[rgba(26,26,26,0.1)] bg-white px-4 py-2 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[rgba(26,26,26,0.56)] transition-colors hover:bg-[#fffdf9]"\r\n        >\r\n          <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />\r\n          Back to Drill Page\r\n        </Link>\r\n      </div>\r\n      <div className="min-w-0">{content}</div>\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.3 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '/**\r\n * Drill 3.7 · Pronoun Replacement.\r\n */\r\n\r\nimport { useState } from \'react\';\r\nimport { PageHeader } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/components/primitives/PageHeader\';\r\nimport { InteractiveDrillRunner } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/components/interactive-drills/InteractiveDrillRunner\';\r\nimport type { InteractiveDrillItem } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/components/interactive-drills/types\';\r\nimport { DRILL_3_7_QUESTIONS, type PronounReplacementQuestion } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/content/drills.source\';\r\nimport { useUser } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/hooks/useUser\';\r\nimport { useModuleProgress } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/hooks/useModuleProgress\';\r\n\r\nconst protocol = {\r\n  title: \'Pronoun Replacement\',\r\n  goal: \'Turn vague conclusion language into a clean standalone claim.\',\r\n  interaction: \'Click the vague phrase, identify its antecedent, then rewrite the conclusion.\',\r\n  afterSubmit: \'The reveal shows the clean replacement and why it preserves the author’s target.\',\r\n};\r\n\r\nfunction adaptQuestion(q: PronounReplacementQuestion): InteractiveDrillItem {\r\n  return {\r\n    id: q.id,\r\n    mode: \'pronoun_replacement\',\r\n    title: \'Pronoun Replacement\',\r\n    prompt: \'Replace the vague phrase with its antecedent.\',\r\n    payload: q,\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function Drill3_7() {\r\n  const user = useUser();\r\n  const { markDrillComplete } = useModuleProgress(user?.id);\r\n  const [qIndex, setQIndex] = useState(0);\r\n  const q = DRILL_3_7_QUESTIONS[qIndex]!;\r\n  const isLast = qIndex + 1 >= DRILL_3_7_QUESTIONS.length;\r\n\r\n  function next() {\r\n    if (isLast) {\r\n      markDrillComplete?.(\'MC-DRL-3.7\');\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n    setQIndex((prev) => prev + 1);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <article className="mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-5 px-6 py-12 desktop:px-12 desktop:py-16">\r\n      <PageHeader\r\n        eyebrow="Drill 3.7"\r\n        title="Pronoun Replacement"\r\n        description="Replace the vague pronoun or referential phrase before you trust the conclusion."\r\n      />\r\n      <InteractiveDrillRunner\r\n        item={adaptQuestion(q)}\r\n        protocol={protocol}\r\n        state={{\r\n          answered: false,\r\n          correct: null,\r\n          selectedAnswer: null,\r\n          questionIndex: qIndex,\r\n          totalQuestions: DRILL_3_7_QUESTIONS.length,\r\n        }}\r\n        onComplete={next}\r\n        completeLabel={isLast ? \'Finish drill\' : \'Next\'}\r\n      />\r\n    </article>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n'}]
